### [40] [Rethinking Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval: Evaluating and Advancing Retrievers in Agentic Search Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04018)
*Yilun Zhao,Jinbiao Wei,Tingyu Song,Siyue Zhang,Chen Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BRIGHT-Pro基准和RTriever模型，用于评估和提升检索器在推理密集型检索任务中的表现，特别是在智能体搜索场景下，通过方面感知和智能体评估方法揭示了标准指标难以发现的检索行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理密集型检索工作在评估和训练两方面均存在局限：基准如BRIGHT仅提供狭窄的黄金证据集并孤立评估检索器，而合成训练语料通常优化单段相关性而非证据组合构建。因此，需要更全面的评估基准和训练方法。

Method: 构建BRIGHT-Pro专家注释基准，为每个查询扩展多方面的黄金证据，并在静态和智能体搜索协议下评估检索器。同时构建RTriever-Synth方面分解的合成语料，生成互补正样本和基于正样本条件的硬负样本，利用LoRA微调Qwen3-Embedding-4B得到RTriever-4B。

Result: 实验表明，方面感知和智能体评估暴露了标准指标隐藏的检索行为，RTriever-4B相比基座模型有显著提升。

Conclusion: 当前推理密集型检索的评估和训练方法需要改进，方面感知和智能体化的评估范式能更真实反映检索器能力，RTriever系列模型为后续研究提供了有效基线。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive retrieval aims to surface evidence that supports downstream reasoning rather than merely matching topical similarity. This capability is increasingly important for agentic search systems, where retrievers must provide complementary evidence across iterative search and synthesis. However, existing work remains limited on both evaluation and training: benchmarks such as BRIGHT provide narrow gold sets and evaluate retrievers in isolation, while synthetic training corpora often optimize single-passage relevance rather than evidence portfolio construction. We introduce BRIGHT-Pro, an expert-annotated benchmark that expands each query with multi-aspect gold evidence and evaluates retrievers under both static and agentic search protocols. We further construct RTriever-Synth, an aspect-decomposed synthetic corpus that generates complementary positives and positive-conditioned hard negatives, and use it to LoRA fine-tune RTriever-4B from Qwen3-Embedding-4B. Experiments across lexical, general-purpose, and reasoning-intensive retrievers show that aspect-aware and agentic evaluation expose behaviors hidden by standard metrics, while RTriever-4B substantially improves over its base model.

</details>


### [41] [Safety and accuracy follow different scaling laws in clinical large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04039)
*Sebastian Wind,Tri-Thien Nguyen,Jeta Sopa,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sebastian Bickelhaup,Michael Uder,Harald Köstler,Gerhard Wellein,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SaFE-Scale框架和RadSaFE-200基准，系统评估临床大语言模型在不同规模、证据质量、检索策略和推理计算下的安全表现。研究发现，干净证据能显著提升准确率并降低高风险错误，而RAG和推理计算扩展并未带来相应的安全改善，表明临床LLM安全并非规模扩展的副产品，而是部署策略的综合结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为通过扩大模型规模、上下文长度、检索复杂度或推理计算即可提升临床LLM的准确性和安全性，但这种假设在医学场景下是片面的——少数高自信、高风险或与证据矛盾的错误可能比平均基准性能更重要。因此需要系统性的安全评估框架。

Method: 提出SaFE-Scale框架，并构建RadSaFE-200基准（200道放射学多选题，含医生标注的干净证据、冲突证据及高风险错误标签）。在34个本地部署的LLM上测试6种部署条件：零样本闭卷、干净证据、冲突证据、标准RAG、智能体RAG和最大上下文提示。

Result: 干净证据将平均准确率从73.5%提升至94.1%，高风险错误从12.0%降至2.6%，矛盾从12.7%降至2.3%，危险过度自信从8.0%降至1.6%。标准RAG和智能体RAG未能复现此安全提升。最大上下文提示增加了延迟但未消除安全差距，额外推理计算仅带来有限改进。

Conclusion: 临床LLM的安全性并非规模扩展的被动结果，而是由证据质量、检索设计、上下文构建和集体失败行为共同决定的部署属性。安全不能仅靠扩展来保证。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs are often scaled by increasing model size, context length, retrieval complexity, or inference-time compute, with the implicit expectation that higher accuracy implies safer behavior. This assumption is incomplete in medicine, where a few confident, high-risk, or evidence-contradicting errors can matter more than average benchmark performance. We introduce SaFE-Scale, a framework for measuring how clinical LLM safety changes across model scale, evidence quality, retrieval strategy, context exposure, and inference-time compute. To instantiate this framework, we introduce RadSaFE-200, a Radiology Safety-Focused Evaluation benchmark of 200 multiple-choice questions with clinician-defined clean evidence, conflict evidence, and option-level labels for high-risk error, unsafe answer, and evidence contradiction. We evaluated 34 locally deployed LLMs across six deployment conditions: closed-book prompting (zero-shot), clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Clean evidence produced the strongest improvement, increasing mean accuracy from 73.5% to 94.1%, while reducing high-risk error from 12.0% to 2.6%, contradiction from 12.7% to 2.3%, and dangerous overconfidence from 8.0% to 1.6%. Standard RAG and agentic RAG did not reproduce this safety profile: agentic RAG improved accuracy over standard RAG and reduced contradiction, but high-risk error and dangerous overconfidence remained elevated. Max-context prompting increased latency without closing the safety gap, and additional inference-time compute produced only limited gains. Worst-case analysis showed that clinically consequential errors concentrated in a small subset of questions. Clinical LLM safety is therefore not a passive consequence of scaling, but a deployment property shaped by evidence quality, retrieval design, context construction, and collective failure behavior.

</details>

### [42] [eOptShrinkQ: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression Through Optimal Spectral Denoising and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02905)
*Pei-Chun Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出eOptShrinkQ，一种基于随机矩阵理论的两阶段KV缓存压缩方法。首先通过最优奇异值收缩提取低秩共享上下文，然后对残差进行近最优量化，在无需异常值处理和偏差校正的情况下，实现了比现有方法更好的压缩-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer注意力头中的KV缓存存在低秩共享结构，现有量化方法未充分利用这一统计特性，且需要额外的异常值处理和偏差校正。作者希望利用随机矩阵理论提供理论保证的压缩方案。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) 使用eOptShrink（最优奇异值收缩）自动提取低秩共享上下文，通过BBP相变自动选择秩；(2) 对满足薄壳性质和坐标离域化的残差使用TurboQuant进行逐向量标量量化。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上验证：每项节省近1比特；LongBench（16项任务）上~2.2比特优于TurboQuant的3.0比特；多针检索任务中2.2比特接近或超过未压缩FP16。

Conclusion: 谱去噪不仅消除了异常值处理和偏差校正的需求，还能作为检索密集型任务的有益正则化器，理论上提供了三种保证：自动秩选择、近零内积偏差、近最优量化失真。

Abstract: We show that the key-value (KV) cache in transformer attention heads admits a natural decomposition into a low-rank \emph{shared context} component and a full-rank \emph{per-token} residual, well described by the spiked random matrix model. This observation leads to eOptShrinkQ, a two-stage compression pipeline: optimal singular value shrinkage (eOptShrink) automatically extracts the shared structure, and the residual -- which satisfies the \emph{thin shell property} with delocalized coordinates -- is quantized by TurboQuant~\citep{zandieh2025turboquant}, a recently proposed per-vector scalar quantizer with near-optimal distortion guarantees. By restoring the isotropy that scalar quantization assumes, spectral denoising eliminates the need for both outlier handling and dedicated inner product bias correction, freeing those bits for improved reconstruction.
  The theoretical grounding in random matrix theory provides three guarantees: automatic rank selection via the BBP phase transition, provably near-zero inner product bias on the residual, and coordinate delocalization ensuring near-optimal quantization distortion. Experimentally, we validate eOptShrinkQ on Llama-3.1-8B and Ministral-8B across three levels: per-head MSE and inner product fidelity, where eOptShrinkQ saves nearly one bit per entry over TurboQuant at equivalent quality; end-to-end on LongBench (16 tasks), where eOptShrinkQ at $\sim$2.2 bits per entry outperforms TurboQuant at 3.0 bits; and multi-needle retrieval, where eOptShrinkQ at 2.2 bits closely matches or exceeds uncompressed FP16, suggesting that spectral denoising can act as a beneficial regularizer for retrieval-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [43] [An End-to-End Framework for Building Large Language Models for Software Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02906)
*Jingkai He,Pengfei Chen,Chenghui Wu,Shuang Liang,Ye Li,Gou Tan,Xiadao Wen,Chuanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpsLLM是一个面向软件运维领域的专用大语言模型，通过人工反馈机制构建高质量数据集、监督微调和领域过程奖励模型，在QA和RCA任务上显著优于现有开源和闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件运维研究因数据质量低、知识碎片化、学习不充分，未能实现高效端到端智能运维，需探索LLM在该领域的潜力。

Method: 首先引入Human-in-the-Loop机制从原始运维数据中筛选高质量数据构建微调数据集；然后进行监督微调得到基础模型；最后引入领域过程奖励模型(DPRM)在强化学习阶段优化RCA任务的准确性和可靠性。

Result: 在QA任务上提升0.2%~5.7%，在RCA任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，并展现出强迁移能力。开源7B、14B和32B三个版本及15K微调数据集。

Conclusion: OpsLLM有效学习并对齐了运维领域知识，在准确性和可靠性上优于现有模型，为LLM在软件运维领域的应用提供了可复现的工作流程。

Abstract: In the field of software operations, Large Language Models (LLMs) have attracted increasing attention. However, existing research has not yet achieved efficient and effective end-to-end intelligent operations due to low-quality data, fragmented knowledge and insufficient learning. To explore the potential of LLMs in software operations, we propose OpsLLM, a domain-specific LLM that supports both knowledge-based question answering (QA) and root cause analysis (RCA). Moreover, we disclose the detailed workflow for building LLMs specifically in the software operations domain. First, a Human-in-the-Loop mechanism is introduced to curate highquality data from a large collection of operational raw data and construct a fine-tuning dataset. Then, based on the data, supervised fine-tuning is conducted to achieve a base model. Furthermore, we introduce a domain process reward model (DPRM) during the reinforcement learning stage to optimize the accuracy and reliability of the fine-tuned model on RCA tasks. Experimental results on the tasks with diverse difficulties demonstrate that OpsLLMs effectively learns and aligns with the operational domain knowledge infused, outperforming existing open-source and closed-source LLMs in accuracy with improvements of 0.2%~5.7% on QA tasks and 2.7% ~70.3% on RCA tasks, while exhibiting strong transferability. Moreover, we will open-source three versions of OpsLLM with 7B, 14B and 32B parameters, along with a 15K fine-tuning dataset.

</details>


### [44] [On the Invariants of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02907)
*Wonsuk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Softmax注意力机制中的能量场（行中心化注意力logit）具有不变性质，包括机制层面的零和约束、秩界和谱特征，以及模型层面的去局域化现象，后者源于键矩阵的非相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力机制将每个查询-键交互映射为概率分布，但其底层结构尚未被充分探索。本文旨在揭示该机制中隐藏的不变性质。

Method: 定义能量场为行中心化的注意力logit，从代数和经验两个层面分析其不变性质。机制层面通过数学推导证明零和约束、秩界和谱特征；模型层面通过大量自回归语言模型实验发现去局域化现象，并追溯至键矩阵的非相干性。

Result: 发现两类不变性质：机制层面的零和约束、秩界和谱特征；模型层面的方差去局域化。秩界将能量场限制在低维子空间，键非相干性可作为每头训练监控指标。

Conclusion: Softmax注意力中存在可解释的不变结构，这些性质具有实际应用价值，如低维约束和训练监控。所有结果在多种上下文长度和输入文本下得到验证。

Abstract: Softmax attention maps every query--key interaction into a probability distribution, but the underlying structure remains largely unexplored. We define the \emph{energy field}, the row-centered attention logit, and show that it exhibits invariant properties across models, architectures, and inputs.
  Two classes of invariants emerge. \emph{Mechanism-level} invariants follow from the algebraic structure of softmax attention. They include a per-row zero-sum constraint, a rank bound determined by the head dimension, and spectral signatures that follow from them. \emph{Model-level} regularities are not required by the mechanism, yet hold in every autoregressive language model we test, spanning several architecture families. The energy field distributes its variance over key positions without concentrating at a few. This delocalization traces to a property of the key matrix we call \emph{key incoherence}.
  These invariants have practical consequences. The rank bound confines the energy field to a low-dimensional subspace. Key incoherence yields a per-head training monitor. All results are verified at multiple context lengths and input texts.

</details>


### [45] [Delay, Plateau, or Collapse: Evaluating the Impact of Systematic Verification Error on RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909)
*Kazuki Egashira,Mark Vero,Jasper Dekoninck,Florian E. Dorner,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）中系统性验证错误的影响，发现系统性假阳性错误会导致性能下降甚至崩溃，且影响取决于错误模式而非整体错误率，挑战了此前认为错误仅减慢训练的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将验证器错误视为随机独立噪声，认为其仅减慢训练。但实际验证器（如静态代码检查器）存在系统性错误，可能让模型学习到不良的持续性行为，需要系统研究这种系统性错误对RLVR的影响。

Method: 在算术任务上进行受控实验，分别研究系统性假阴性错误和系统性假阳性错误对RLVR训练的影响。

Result: 系统性假阴性错误的影响类似于随机噪声；而系统性假阳性错误会导致从次优平台到性能崩溃的多种不良结果。关键发现是，这些结果不由总体错误率决定，而是由错误的具体模式决定。

Conclusion: 与以往结论不同，现实中的验证器错误会严重影响RLVR的训练结果，验证器质量评估不能仅看样本级错误率，还需关注错误的系统性模式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance. However, practical verifiers tend to exhibit systematic errors. This introduces a risk of models learning unwanted consistent behavior from a structurally incorrect reward signal. In this work, we study the impact of such systematic verification errors on RLVR. Through controlled experiments on arithmetic tasks, we show that systematic false negatives lead to similar effects as random noise. On the other hand, systematic false positives can cause a wide range of behaviors from sub-optimal plateaus to performance collapse. Crucially, these outcomes are not determined by the overall error rate but by the specific pattern of introduced errors, making pre-hoc mitigation difficult. Our results show that, in contrast to prior conclusions, realistic verification errors can critically shape RLVR outcomes and that verifier quality has to be understood beyond its sample-level error rate.

</details>


### [46] [Generate, Filter, Control, Replay: A Comprehensive Survey of Rollout Strategies for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02913)
*Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Xunyi Jiang,Chuhan Wang,Zhenwei Tang,Difan Jiao,Zihan Huang,Yuxin Xiong,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Xintong Li,Raghav Jain,Nikki Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Bowen Jin,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Ryan Rossi,Kuan-Hao Huang,Jingbo Shang,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地综述了强化学习后训练中，大语言模型推理阶段的 rollout 策略，提出了 GFCR 生命周期分类法和可靠性、覆盖率、成本敏感性三个评估维度，涵盖数学、代码、多模态推理和工具使用 agent 等应用场景，并提供了常见病理的诊断索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rollout 设计决定了强化学习优化器所学习的数据质量，但现有文献对这一环节的报告严重不足。作者旨在提供一种与优化器无关的视角，系统梳理和形式化 rollout 策略，填补该领域的系统性缺失。

Method: 提出 GFCR 四阶段分类法（生成-过滤-控制-回放），统一符号形式化 rollout 流水线；构建可靠性、覆盖率、成本敏感性三个评估准则；通过案例研究在数学、代码/SQL、多模态推理、工具型 agent 等场景中验证框架适用性。

Result: 系统性地梳理并归类了现有方法，包括可验证奖励 RL、过程监督、judge 门控、导向式和树/段式 rollout、自适应计算分配、早退与部分 rollout、吞吐量优化、以及基于回放/重组的自我改进等。提供了 rollout 病理诊断索引。

Conclusion: Rollout 设计对 RL 后训练效果至关重要，GFCR 框架为理解和优化 rollout 提供了统一工具；未来需解决可复现、计算高效和可信赖的 rollout 流水线建设等开放挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training tool for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs). In these systems, the rollout, the trajectory sampled from a prompt to termination, including intermediate reasoning steps and optional tool or environment interactions, determines the data the optimizer learns from, yet rollout design is often underreported. This survey provides an optimizer-agnostic view of rollout strategies for RL-based post-training of reasoning LLMs. We formalize rollout pipelines with unified notation and introduce Generate-Filter-Control-Replay (GFCR), a lifecycle taxonomy that decomposes rollout pipelines into four modular stages: Generate proposes candidate trajectories and topologies; Filter constructs intermediate signals via verifiers, judges, critics; Control allocates compute and makes continuation/branching/stopping decisions under budgets; and Replay retains and reuses artifacts across rollouts without weight updates, including self-evolving curricula that autonomously generate new training tasks. We complement GFCR with a criterion taxonomy of reliability, coverage, and cost sensitivity that characterizes rollout trade-offs. Using this framework, we synthesize methods spanning RL with verifiable rewards, process supervision, judge-based gating, guided and tree/segment rollouts, adaptive compute allocation, early-exit and partial rollouts, throughput optimization, and replay/recomposition for self-improvement. We ground the framework with case studies in math, code/SQL, multimodal reasoning, tool-using agents, and agentic skill benchmarks that evaluate skill induction, reuse, and cross-task transfer. Finally, we provide a diagnostic index that maps common rollout pathologies to GFCR modules and mitigation levers, alongside open challenges for building reproducible, compute-efficient, and trustworthy rollout pipelines.

</details>


### [47] [When Safety Geometry Collapses: Fine-Tuning Vulnerabilities in Agentic Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02914)
*Ismail Hossain,Sai Puppala,Jannatul Ferdaus,Md Jahangir Alam,Yoonpyo Lee,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对完全良性数据进行微调的防护模型会丧失安全对齐能力，其根源在于安全表征几何结构的崩塌。本文提出Fisher加权安全子空间正则化（FW-SSR）方法，可恢复75%的拒答率，并将攻击成功率降至3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现通用大语言模型在良性微调后会失去安全对齐，但防护模型（专门用于安全分类的模型）是否也存在同样问题尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示防护模型在领域特化过程中安全能力的崩塌机制，并提出有效的缓解方法。

Method: 1）通过SVD提取逐层安全子空间，追踪良性微调下安全表征边界（有害-良性）的演化；2）提出FW-SSR正则化方法，结合Fisher信息对角线曲率感知方向权重和自适应λt（随任务-安全梯度冲突缩放）。

Result: Granite Guardian拒答率从85%降至0%，CKA归零，100%输出模糊；FW-SSR恢复75%拒答率（CKA=0.983），WildGuard攻击成功率降至3.6%（低于未修改基线）。

Conclusion: 防护模型的安全表征存在"集中性脆弱"特性，结构化的表征几何度量（CKA、Fisher分数）比绝对位移度量更能可靠预测安全行为，几何监测应成为防护模型评估的必要组成部分。

Abstract: A guard model fine-tuned on entirely benign data can lose all safety alignment -- not through adversarial manipulation, but through standard domain specialization. We demonstrate this failure across three purpose-built safety classifiers -- LlamaGuard, WildGuard, and Granite Guardian -- deployed as protection layers in agentic AI pipelines, and show that it originates in the destruction of latent safety geometry: the structured harmful -- benign representational boundary that guides classification. We extract per-layer safety subspaces via SVD on class-conditional activation differences and track how this boundary evolves under benign fine-tuning. Granite Guardian undergoes complete collapse --  refusal rate drops from 85\% to 0\%, CKA falls to zero, and 100\% of outputs become ambiguous -- a severity exceeding prior findings on general-purpose LLMs, explained by the specialization hypothesis: concentrated safety representations are efficient but catastrophically brittle. To mitigate this, we propose Fisher-Weighted Safety Subspace Regularization (FW-SSR), a training-time penalty combining (i) curvature-aware direction weights derived from diagonal Fisher information and (ii) an adaptive $λ_t$ that scales with task-safety gradient conflict. FW-SSR recovers 75\% refusal on Granite Guardian (CKA = 0.983) and reduces WildGuard's Attack Success Rate to 3.6\% -- below the unmodified baseline -- by actively sharpening the safety subspace rather than merely anchoring it. Across all three models, structural representational geometry (CKA, Fisher score) predicts safety behavior more reliably than absolute displacement metrics, establishing geometry-based monitoring as a necessary component of guard model evaluation in agentic deployments.

</details>


### [48] [From Synthesis to Clinical Assistance: A Strategy-Aware Agent Framework for Autism Intervention based on Real Clinical Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02916)
*Junhong Lai,Shuzhong Lai,Yanhao Yu,Wanlin Chen,Chenyu Yan,Haifeng Li,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASDAgent框架，通过策略感知的对话生成与行为建模，解决了AI辅助孤独症早期干预中的数据稀缺与策略不一致问题，生成的干预对话与人类治疗师的策略分布高度一致（KL散度0.083），并能将专业知识蒸馏至小语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助孤独症早期干预面临两大瓶颈：一是数据极度稀缺，二是通用大语言模型难以严格遵循应用行为分析（ABA）的标准化流程，容易出现“语言流畅但策略不一致”的问题。

Method: 提出ASDAgent框架，包含两个核心组件：1) DoctorAgent，采用观察-思考-行动-纠正（O-T-A-C）推理循环，使ABA执行显式可控；2) ChildAgent，利用概率行为建模模拟多样化的孤独症响应模式，缓解数据同质性问题。

Result: ASDAgent生成的对话与人类治疗师的策略分布KL散度仅为0.083；在实际孤独症干预中，与人类专家的策略一致性接近80%；合成数据可有效将专业知识蒸馏至小语言模型，显著提升其治疗能力。

Conclusion: ASDAgent通过策略感知框架统一了高保真干预对话合成与临床决策支持，为解决AI辅助孤独症干预中的数据稀缺与策略遵循问题提供了有效方案，并展示了合成数据在小模型知识蒸馏中的潜力。

Abstract: The development of AI-assisted Early Intensive Behavioral Intervention (EIBI) for Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is severely constrained by data scarcity. Furthermore, while Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) serves as the gold standard for clinical intervention, general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to strictly adhere to its standardized procedures, often resulting in interactions that are linguistically fluent but strategically inconsistent. To address these challenges, we introduce \textsc{ASDAgent}, a strategy-aware framework designed to unify high-fidelity intervention dialogue synthesis and clinical decision support. \textsc{ASDAgent} incorporates two specialized components to solve distinct problems: (i) a \textsc{DoctorAgent} equipped with an Observe-Think-Act-Correct (O-T-A-C) reasoning loop, which resolves the issue of strategy collapse in LLMs by making ABA execution explicit and controllable; and (ii) a \textsc{ChildAgent} that utilizes probabilistic behavior modeling to mitigate data homogeneity, simulating diverse and non-deterministic ASD response patterns. Experiments demonstrate that dialogues generated by \textsc{ASDAgent} closely mirror the strategy distribution of human therapists (KL divergence: 0.083). In real autism intervention, \textsc{ASDAgent} achieves nearly 80\% strategic consistency with human experts. Moreover, we show that synthetic data produced by \textsc{ASDAgent} effectively distills professional clinical knowledge into small language models (SLMs), significantly enhancing their therapeutic capabilities.

</details>


### [49] [Mitigating the reconstruction-detection trade-off in VAE-based unsupervised anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02918)
*Agathe Senellart,Maëlys Solal,Stéphanie Allassonnière,Ninon Burgos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: β-VAE模型在重建质量与异常检测之间存在权衡：潜在空间受限的模型检测性能更好但重建质量下降。论文研究了Beta调度和稀疏VAE两种缓解该权衡的方法，其中稀疏VAE在保持高重建质量的同时提升了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测中变分自编码器的超参数选择仍是一个开放问题，目前常以正常样本的重建误差最小化为标准，但该方法与检测性能之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: 对β-VAE模型进行系统性分析，揭示重建质量与异常检测之间的权衡关系；评估不同随机种子下的性能变异性，并将其与正常/异常潜在分布的距离关联起来；提出并研究Beta调度和稀疏VAE两种缓解方法。

Result: 发现了β-VAE模型中重建质量与异常检测性能的权衡关系；证明了随机种子导致的性能变异与正常和异常潜在分布之间的距离有关；稀疏VAE在保持高重建质量的同时显著提升了检测性能。

Conclusion: 稀疏VAE是缓解重建-检测权衡的有效方法，能够在不牺牲重建质量的前提下提升无监督异常检测的效果。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders are widely used for unsupervised anomaly detection. Model selection however remains an open-question: to remain fully unsupervised, hyperparameters are often chosen to minimize the reconstruction error on normal samples. In this paper, we reveal a trade-off between reconstruction quality and anomaly detection among $β$-VAE models. Models with constrained latent space reach higher detection metrics but lower reconstruction quality. We also assess the performance variability across random seeds and show it is linked to the distance between normal and abnormal latent distributions. From this analysis, we justify and investigate two methods to mitigate the reconstructiondetection tradeoff: beta-scheduling and the Sparse VAE. The latter especially shows an improvement in detection while maintaining high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [50] [DeRelayL: Sustainable Decentralized Relay Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02935)
*Haihan Duan,Tengfei Ma,Yuyang Qin,Runhao Zeng,Wei Cai,Victor C. M. Leung,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为去中心化中继学习（DeRelayL）的新型协作训练范式，允许无权限参与者以中继方式贡献模型训练并共享模型，旨在解决大规模模型训练中普通用户参与门槛高、可持续性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模模型训练需要高昂的计算和财务资源，只有少数科技巨头能够负担，而普通用户（如移动用户）虽然是数据的创造者却被排除在外。现有访问大规模模型的方法要么限制用户所有权，要么缺乏可持续性。现有协作训练范式（如联邦学习）主要关注数据隐私和基于群体的模型聚合，无法解决上述问题。

Method: 本文提出去中心化中继学习（DeRelayL）范式，其架构和工作流程允许无权限参与者以中继方式贡献模型训练并共享模型，同时设计了激励机制来确保系统的可持续性。

Result: 通过理论分析和数值模拟验证了所提DeRelayL方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DeRelayL作为一种可持续的学习系统，能够使普通用户参与大规模模型训练并共享模型，弥合了当前大模型训练中的参与差距。

Abstract: In the era of big data, large-scale machine learning models have revolutionized various fields, driving significant advancements. However, large-scale model training demands high financial and computational resources, which are only affordable by a few technological giants and well-funded institutions. In this case, common users like mobile users, the real creators of valuable data, are often excluded from fully benefiting due to the barriers, while the current methods for accessing large-scale models either limit user ownership or lack sustainability. This growing gap highlights the urgent need for a collaborative model training approach, allowing common users to train and share models. However, existing collaborative model training paradigms, especially federated learning (FL), primarily focus on data privacy and group-based model aggregation. To this end, this paper intends to address this issue by proposing a novel training paradigm named decentralized relay learning (DeRelayL), a sustainable learning system where permissionless participants can contribute to model training in a relay-like manner and share the model. In detail, this paper presents the architecture and workflow of DeRelayL, designs incentive mechanisms to ensure sustainability, and conducts theoretical analysis and numerical simulations to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [51] [Proteo-R1: Reasoning Foundation Models for De Novo Protein Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02937)
*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Heli Qi,Hanqun Cao,Heng-Jui Chang,Zeqi Zhou,Haokai Zhao,Ma Jian,Carl Ma,Yu-Chi Cheng,Kuan Pang,Xiangru Tang,Zehong Wang,Guanlue Li,Hanchen Wang,Kejun Ying,Pan Lu,Chiho Im,Seungju Han,Peng Xia,Tinson Xu,Yinxi Li,Deyao Zhu,Pheng-Ann Heng,Naoto Yokoya,Masashi Sugiyama,Li Erran Li,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proteo-R1 将蛋白质设计解耦为“理解专家”（MLLM 分析关键残基）和“生成专家”（扩散模型条件生成），实现可解释、可控的蛋白质设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质深度学习方法缺乏推理能力，设计决策与连续采样纠缠，导致可解释性差、可控性低，且难以系统复用生化知识。

Method: 采用双专家架构：多模态大语言模型作为理解专家，识别关键功能残基作为硬约束；扩散模型作为生成专家，在这些约束下进行条件共设计。

Result: 实现了稳定、可解释且模块化的蛋白质设计，将LLM推理与几何生成有效结合。

Conclusion: 将推理显式操作为残基级别的硬约束而非隐式文本引导，能更好地模仿人类专家先推理再优化的设计范式。

Abstract: Deep learning in \emph{de novo} protein design has achieved atomic-level fidelity. However, existing models remain largely non-deliberative: they directly synthesize molecular geometries without explicitly reasoning about which residues or interactions are functionally essential. As a result, design decisions are entangled with continuous sampling dynamics, limiting interpretability, controllability, and systematic reuse of biochemical knowledge. We introduce \textbf{Proteo-R1}, a reasoning-guided protein design framework that explicitly decouples \emph{molecular understanding} from \emph{geometric generation}. Proteo-R1 adopts a dual-expert architecture in which a multimodal large language model (MLLM) serves as an \emph{understanding expert}, analyzing protein sequences, structures, and textual context to identify key functional residues that govern binding and specificity. These residue-level decisions are then passed as hard constraints to a separate diffusion-based \emph{generation expert}, which performs conditional co-design while respecting the fixed interaction anchors. This factorization mirrors how human experts approach molecular engineering: first, reasoning about critical interactions, then optimizing geometry subject to those constraints. By operationalizing reasoning as explicit residue-level commitments rather than latent textual guidance, Proteo-R1 achieves stable, interpretable, and modular integration of LLM reasoning with state-of-the-art geometric generative models. Code, data, and demos are available at https://smiles724.github.io/r1/.

</details>


### [52] [From Static Analysis to Audience Dissemination: A Training-Free Multimodal Controversy Detection Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02939)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuDisAgent，一个无需训练的多智能体框架，将多模态争议检测从静态表示学习重新定义为动态传播过程，通过模拟受众讨论机制显著提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将MCD视为静态表示学习任务，直接提取视频和评论的特征，无法捕捉不同受众群体的多元视角和评价，忽视了内容在传播过程中涌现的争议性。

Method: 构建多智能体系统：1）三个筛选智能体（视频、评论、交互）进行初步评估；2）不一致时激活观众座谈智能体，模拟不同背景受众的讨论；3）仲裁智能体基于完整推理链做出最终判断。另设计评论引导策略解决冷启动问题。

Result: 在公开数据集上，该方法在丰富评论和有限评论场景下均显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过模拟真实的受众传播与讨论过程，能够有效捕捉潜在争议内容，该方法无需训练即可部署，且能解决新视频的冷启动问题。

Abstract: Multimodal controversy detection (MCD) identifies controversial content in videos and their associated user comments, to support risk management for social video platforms.Prior research frames MCD as a static representation learning task, where features are directly extracted from videos and their accompanying comments. However, these methods fail to capture the diverse perspectives and evaluations from different audience groups. Inspired by the real-world process of content dissemination among audiences, we propose AuDisAgent, a training-free multi-agent framework that reformulates MCD as a dynamic propagation process.Our framework explicitly models audience dissemination through a structured multi-agent system. First, three specialized Screening Agents (Video Agent, Comment Agent, and Interaction Agent) conduct initial assessments from visual, textual, and cross-modal perspectives, respectively. For samples where the three agents cannot reach a consensus, a Viewing Panel Agent is activated to simulate post-screening discussions among audiences with diverse backgrounds and stances. This mechanism models how different audience groups interpret and react to the same content, uncovering latent controversial content that may emerge during the dissemination process. Finally, an Arbitration Agent renders the final judgment based on the complete reasoning chain from the preceding steps.In addition, to address the "cold-start" scenario where newly released videos have few or no comments, we design a Comment Bootstrapping Strategy that leverages historical public comments from semantically similar videos as the initial comment context. Extensive experiments on a public dataset demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in both rich-comment and limited-comment scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [PrismAgent: Illuminating Harm in Memes via a Zero-Shot Interpretable Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02940)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PrismAgent，一种零样本、多智能体、可解释的框架，通过模拟刑事案件调查流程（分析、调查、起诉、审判）来实现迷因有害内容检测，在三个公开数据集上显著优于现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迷因有害内容检测方法依赖大量标注数据，训练成本高且泛化能力有限。

Method: 将任务构建为刑事案件调查流程，包含四个专业智能体：分析智能体（从善意和恶意假设重构迷因）、调查智能体（从未标注数据中检索证据并构建上下文解释）、起诉智能体（将原始迷因与三种解释配对进行初步判断）、法官智能体（综合所有证据做出最终裁决）。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，PrismAgent显著优于现有零样本检测方法。

Conclusion: PrismAgent通过多阶段推理链实现了零样本下的可解释有害内容检测，有效降低了训练成本并提升了泛化能力。

Abstract: The rapid spread of memes makes harmful content detection increasingly crucial, as effective identification can curb the circulation of misinformation. However, existing methods rely heavily on high-volume annotated data, which leads to substantial training costs and limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose PrismAgent, a zero-shot, multi-agent, interpretable framework. PrismAgent conceptualizes this task as a criminal case investigation, employing four specialized agents responsible for the analysis, investigation, prosecution, and judgment stages within a structured collaborative workflow. In the first stage, the analyst agent paraphrases each meme under benevolent and malicious assumptions to probe its underlying intent. The investigator agent then retrieves supporting evidence from an unannotated dataset and constructs contextual interpretations for the meme and its variants. Next, the prosecutor agent performs three independent preliminary judgments by pairing the original meme with each of the three interpretations. Finally, the judge agent deliberates across all evidence to render a final verdict. Moreover, PrismAgent's explicit multi-stage reasoning chain makes the model inherently interpretable, as every intermediate step is explicitly explained rather than only producing a final detection result. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that PrismAgent significantly outperforms existing zero-shot detection methods.

</details>

*Aya Elgebaly,Joris Fournel,Benjamin Laine Jønch Jurgensen,Kamil Mikolaj,Anders Christensen,Martin Tolsgaard,Claes Ladefoged,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构化框架，用于探索和检测医学AI中的交叉偏见。通过对超过94,000张胎儿超声图像进行案例分析，发现像素间距（PS）是影响模型性能差异的关键因素，且与孕周（GA）和BMI等变量存在混杂效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI偏见研究多聚焦于数据代表性不足，但即便数据分布均衡，图像质量等因素仍可能导致性能差异，且这些因素可能与人口统计学特征相关，形成更复杂的交互与混杂效应。

Method: 结合无监督切片发现、系统因子分析和定向交叉评估，构建了一个结构化框架；在94,000+张胎儿超声图像上对比评估深度学习模型与临床Hadlock标准。

Result: 像素间距（PS）是性能差异的关键驱动因素，高PS在特定亚组中带来高达24%的改进；GA部分解释了PS相关信号，而PS的改善效应在BMI各层中持续存在。

Conclusion: 医学AI公平性研究需要纳入采集意识和交互意识的评估方法，不能仅关注数据代表性，还需考虑图像质量、操作条件等临床实践因素。

Abstract: Bias in medical AI is often framed as a problem of representation. However, in image-based tasks such as fetal ultrasound, performance disparities can arise even when representation is adequate, because predictive accuracy depends strongly on image quality. Image quality is shaped by acquisition conditions and operator expertise, as well as patient-dependent factors such as maternal body mass index (BMI), all of which may correlate with sensitive demographic features. Consequently, observed disparities may reflect the combined influence of demographic, clinical, and acquisition-related factors rather than data imbalance alone, and may obscure underlying interaction or confounding effects. We propose a structured framework to explore and detect intersectional bias, combining unsupervised slice discovery, systematic factor-wise analysis, and targeted intersectional evaluation. In a case study of over 94{,}000 ultrasound images for fetal weight estimation, we analyze bias in a state-of-the-art deep learning (DL) model and the clinical standard Hadlock, a regression formula using biometric measurements. Pixel spacing (PS) -- a parameter considered suboptimal in current acquisition protocols -- emerged as a consistent driver of performance differences, with higher PS associated with improvements of up to 24\% in selected subgroups for both models. Because PS is often adapted in cases of high BMI or low gestational age (GA), this effect carries a substantial risk of confounding. Our intersectional analysis revealed that part of the PS-associated signal is explained by GA, while PS-related improvements persist across BMI strata, highlighting the importance of acquisition-aware and interaction-aware evaluation in medical AI fairness research.

</details>


### [55] [Healthcare AI GYM for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02943)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-OPD框架，通过密集的KL正则化解决多轮强化学习中存在的长度爆炸和工具使用退化问题，在18个医疗基准中的10个上取得最优性能，平均提升3.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多轮强化学习训练方法存在两个核心问题：多轮对话退化成长长的单轮独白（工具使用减少、长度爆炸），以及蒸馏不稳定。这些问题源于稀疏的终端奖励与顺序性临床轨迹之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD)，利用无梯度的指数移动平均教师模型，在每个对话轮次提供密集的、结果感知的KL正则化，替代传统的稀疏奖励信号。

Result: 在10个临床领域的18个基准测试中，TT-OPD在10个基准上取得最佳性能，平均提升3.9个百分点；相比普通GRPO，收敛更快、响应长度可控、多轮工具使用得以保持。

Conclusion: TT-OPD通过密集的轮次级KL正则化有效解决了多轮医疗AI强化学习中的训练不稳定和工具使用退化问题，为通用医疗AI智能体的训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning demands multi-step interactions -- gathering patient history, ordering tests, interpreting results, and making safe treatment decisions -- yet a unified training environment provides the breadth of clinical domains and specialized tools to train generalizable medical AI agents through reinforcement learning remains elusive. We present a comprehensive empirical study of multi-turn agentic RL for medical AI, built on \gym{}, a gymnasium-compatible environment spanning 10 clinical domains with 3.6K+ tasks, 135 domain-specific tools, and a knowledge base of 828K medical passages. Our analysis reveals that agentic multi-turn structure degrades into verbose single-turn monologues, characterized by monotonic length explosion and a simultaneous erosion of tool-use frequency. We characterize how this collapse, alongside distillation instability, stems from the misalignment of sparse terminal rewards with sequential clinical trajectories. We find that vanilla GRPO achieves strong final accuracy on some benchmarks but suffers from training instability, evidenced by significant oscillations in response length and prolonged convergence periods. To improve training efficiency and stability, we propose Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD), a self-distillation framework where a gradient-free EMA teacher leverages outcome-privileged information to provide dense, outcome-aware KL regularization at every conversation turn. TT-OPD achieves the best performance on 10 of 18 benchmarks with an average +3.9~pp improvement over the non-RL baseline with faster early convergence, controlled response length, and sustained multi-turn tool use.

</details>


### [56] [Exploring Pass-Rate Reward in Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02944)
*Xin-Ye Li,Ren-Biao Liu,Yun-Ji Zhang,Hui Sun,Zheng Xie,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码生成中无评论家强化学习使用通过率奖励的问题，发现尽管通过率奖励比二元奖励更密集，但并不能可靠地提升最终性能，原因在于通过率奖励的梯度更新方向不一致，无法有效将概率质量推向完全正确的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL中使用全测试通过的二元奖励存在奖励稀疏性问题，在困难问题上无法提供学习信号，因此研究者常使用测试用例通过率作为替代奖励，但其有效性尚未被严谨验证。

Method: 在无评论家RL（如GRPO和RLOO）中，对通过率奖励与二元奖励进行严格控制的对比实验，并分析奖励密度与梯度方向。

Result: 通过率奖励虽更密集，但在多个基模型和算法上并未可靠地超越二元奖励的最终性能；其梯度更新不能持续将概率质量移向完全正确的解，部分正确解间存在冲突梯度相互抵消。

Conclusion: 在无评论家RL中，通过率奖励不足以改进代码生成性能，需要设计更好对齐完全正确目标的奖励机制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) from unit-test feedback has become a standard post-training recipe for improving large language models (LLMs) on code generation. However, the pass-all-tests binary reward can be sparse, yielding no learning signal on challenging problems where none of the sampled solutions passes all tests. A common remedy is to use the test-case pass rate as a surrogate reward.
  In this work, we study pass-rate rewards in critic-free RL for code generation (e.g., GRPO and RLOO) and report a consistent pattern across base models and algorithms: despite alleviating reward sparsity, pass-rate rewards do not reliably improve final performance over binary rewards in rigorous controlled experiments.
  To understand this discrepancy, we analyze reward density and the resulting gradient directions. We find that pass-rate rewards are denser, but the induced gradient updates do not consistently move probability mass toward full-pass solutions. This arises because test-case pass rate is a miscalibrated surrogate for progress toward full correctness, and partial-pass solutions within the same group can induce conflicting gradient directions that cancel out.
  Overall, our results suggest that, in critic-free RL, pass-rate rewards are insufficient to improve code generation and motivate reward designs that better align optimization with the goal of full correctness.

</details>


### [57] [RouteHijack: Routing-Aware Attack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02946)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RouteHijack，一种针对混合专家（MoE）大语言模型的路径感知越狱攻击方法。通过识别并操纵安全相关专家的路由决策，RouteHijack在仅需输入访问权限的情况下，在7个MoE模型上达到69.3%的平均攻击成功率，显著优于现有方法，并展现了良好的跨模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）架构在大模型中广泛应用，但其安全鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究。现有对抗攻击方法存在局限性：基于提示的越狱依赖启发式搜索且迁移性差；模型干预方法需要特权内部表示访问权限；基于优化的输入攻击在MoE模型中因路由机制不可微而受限。因此需要一种专门针对MoE架构的安全攻击方法。

Method: RouteHijack包含两个阶段：1) 响应驱动的专家定位阶段，通过对比安全拒绝和有害完成下的激活值，识别安全关键专家和有害专家；2) 对抗后缀构建阶段，使用路径感知目标函数，抑制安全专家激活、促进有害专家激活，并防止生成早期拒绝。推理时，将优化后的后缀附加到恶意提示后，仅需输入级访问。

Result: 在7个MoE LLM上达到69.3%的平均攻击成功率（ASR），是此前基于优化的攻击方法的3.2倍。零样本迁移至5个同族MoE变体时，ASR从27.7%提升至61.2%；泛化至3个MoE视觉大模型时，ASR从2.47%提升至38.7%。

Conclusion: RouteHijack揭示了稀疏专家架构中存在的根本性安全漏洞：安全行为集中在一小部分专家上，通过操纵路由决策即可绕过安全对齐。这突显了在输出级对齐之外，还需要针对路由机制设计防御策略。

Abstract: Safety alignment is critical for the responsible deployment of large language models (LLMs). As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly adopted to scale model capacity, understanding their safety robustness becomes essential. Existing adversarial attacks, however, have notable limitations. Prompt-based jailbreaks rely on heuristic search and transfer poorly, model intervention methods require privileged access to internal representations, and optimization-based input attacks remain output-centric and are fundamentally limited to MoE models due to the non-differentiable routing mechanism.
  In this paper, we present RouteHijack, a routing-aware jailbreak for MoE LLMs. Our key insight is that safety behavior is concentrated in a small subset of experts, creating an opportunity to steer model behavior by influencing routing decisions through input optimization. Building on this observation, RouteHijack first performs response-driven expert localization to identify safety-critical and harmful experts by contrasting activations under safe refusals and harmful completions. It then constructs adversarial suffixes with a routing-aware objective that suppresses safety experts, promotes harmful experts, and prevents early-stage refusal during generation. At inference time, the optimized suffix is appended to a malicious prompt, requiring only input access. Across seven MoE LLMs, RouteHijack achieves a 69.3\% average attack success rate (ASR), outperforming prior optimization-based attack by $3.2\times$. RouteHijack also transfers zero-shot across five sibling MoE variants, raising average ASR from 27.7\% to 61.2\%, and further generalizes to three MoE-based VLMs, increasing average ASR from 2.47\% to 38.7\%. These findings expose a fundamental vulnerability in sparse expert architectures and highlight the need for defenses beyond output-level alignment.

</details>


### [58] [AsymK-Talker: Real-Time and Long-Horizon Talking Head Generation via Asymmetric Kernel Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02948)
*Yuxin Lu,Qian Qiao,Jiayang Sun,Min Cao,Guibo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsymK-Talker 是一种基于扩散蒸馏的实时长时说话头生成方法，通过核条件循环生成、时序参考编码和非对称核蒸馏三大组件，解决了现有方法在因果效率、时序一致性和长程漂移方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型驱动的说话头生成存在三个关键问题：因果推理效率低导致无法实时推理、难以与时序一致的条件兼容、以及长序列生成中会出现渐进性漂移，这些都阻碍了在实时场景中的应用。

Method: 提出 AsymK-Talker，包含三个组件：(1) 核条件循环生成（KCLG），一种因果的、基于块的生成范式，利用运动核实现时序一致传播；(2) 时序参考编码（TRE），将静态身份参考转化为时序感知的潜在表示以增强视听同步；(3) 非对称核蒸馏（AKD），教师模型基于真实运动核进行监督，学生模型学习从生成核中生成，保证长序列生成的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉保真度和唇形同步指标上均取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: AsymK-Talker 有效克服了现有扩散方法在实时和长时说话头生成中的因果效率、时序一致性和漂移问题，为实现实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have markedly enhanced the visual fidelity of audio-driven talking head generation. Nevertheless, existing methods are constrained by three critical limitations: causal inefficiency that impedes real-time inference, incompatibility with temporally coherent conditioning, and progressive drift over long-horizon generation, collectively hindering their deployment in real-time applications. To overcome these challenges, we introduce AsymK-Talker, a novel diffusion-distillation method designed for real-time and long-horizon talking head generation. AsymK-Talker comprises three key components: (1) Kernel-Conditioned Loop Generation (KCLG), a causal, chunk-wise generation paradigm that leverages motion kernels to enable temporally consistent propagation; (2) Temporal Reference Encoding (TRE), which converts a static identity reference into a time-aware latent representation to enhance audio-visual synchronization; and (3) Asymmetric Kernel Distillation (AKD), a teacher-student distillation framework wherein the teacher model conditions on ground-truth motion kernels for supervision, while the student learns to generate from generated kernels, thereby ensuring robustness during extended generation sequences. AsymK-Talker achieves promising results on both visual fidelity and lip synchronization metrics.

</details>


### [59] [Disease Is a Spectral Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02949)
*John D. Mayfield,Matthew S. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用生物标志物“哈密顿量”建模疾病转化过程的方法，通过分析健康与疾病状态下协方差矩阵的谱变化（特征值与特征向量偏移），实现分子层面和个体层面的疾病轨迹解释，并利用新患者的协方差结构在疾病判别本征模态上的投影进行精准预后预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疾病转化理解缺乏生物标志物层面的可解释性，难以同时在分子层面和个体层面刻画疾病轨迹的机制性解释，亟需一种统一框架来量化疾病扰动对生物标志物协调结构的影响。

Method: 定义生物标志物“哈密顿量”H = X^T X / n，其本征向量定义正常模态，本征值量化模态能量；健康状态由参考哈密顿量H_0描述，疾病扰动通过加法算子ΔH作用于H_0，导致特征值偏移和特征向量旋转；通过新患者累积协方差结构在疾病判别本征模态上的投影进行预后统计推断。

Result: 推导了疾病扰动下的谱变化理论公式；证明新患者协方差结构在疾病判别模态上的投影可作为最优预后统计量，实现更精确的疾病预后；该方法适用于从癌症到神经退行性疾病等多种疾病框架。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解疾病转化提供了具有生物标志物层面可解释性的统一方法，兼具分子机制解释和个体化预后预测的双重能力，是一份具有广泛应用前景的白皮书式工作。

Abstract: We propose a novel method of understanding disease transformation from a healthy baseline with biomarker-level explainability. By modeling the biomarker covariance matrices of healthy controls and disease states, the perturbation can be individually characterized to accomplish mechanistic explanations of disease trajectories, both at a molecular level and for individual patients. Given a cohort of n patients each measured on p biomarkers, we define the biomarker "Hamiltonian" H = X^T X / n \in R^{p \times p}, where X \in R^{n \times p} is the covariant biomarker matrix. The eigenvectors of H define a set of normal modes of biomarker coordination, and the eigenvalues quantify the energy carried by each mode. In the healthy state, the reference Hamiltonian H_0 governs this structure where disease perturbs H_0 by an additive operator ΔH, thus shifting eigenvalues and rotating eigenvectors in proportion to the severity of pathological disruption. We formalize this framework, derive the spectral change given a disease perturbation, and demonstrate that the projection of a newly diagnosed patient's cumulative biomarker covariance structure onto disease-discriminant eigenmodes constitutes an optimal prognostic statistic for greater precision in disease prognosis. This work serves as a veritable white paper with application across a panoply of disease frameworks from cancer to neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [60] [Kernel Affine Hull Machines for Compute-Efficient Query-Side Semantic Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02950)
*Mohit Kumar,Somayeh Kargaran,Bernhard A. Moser,Manuela Geiß*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出核仿射包络机（KAHM），用轻量级几何估计器替代固定教师场景下的在线Transformer查询编码，在保持检索质量的同时将延迟降低8.5倍，并在法律检索基准上取得最优教师空间重建和排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语义检索中在线查询编码的计算成本过高，作者希望用轻量级、可解析的显式估计器替代重复的神经推理，在不损害检索质量的前提下提升效率与可解释性。

Method: 提出KAHM：将廉价的词汇特征映射到冻结的语义嵌入空间，通过RKHS中估计原型混合权重，并利用归一化最小均方算法优化原型，将编码误差分解为后验近似、泛化和教师噪声三个分量。

Result: 在奥地利法律基准上，KAHM取得教师空间重建最优（MSE 0.000091, R² 0.9071, 余弦相似度0.9536），排序指标领先（MRR@20 0.504, Hit@20 0.694, Top-1 0.411），延迟比Transformer编码降低8.5倍。

Conclusion: 在固定教师场景下，轻量级几何估计器可以有效替代在线神经编码，兼顾检索性能、效率与可解释性。

Abstract: Transformer-based semantic retrieval is highly effective, yet in many deployments the dominant cost lies in online query encoding rather than corpus indexing. We study the fixed-teacher query-adaptation problem and ask whether repeated neural inference can be replaced by a lightweight, analytically explicit estimator without degrading decision-relevant retrieval quality. We propose Kernel Affine Hull Machines (KAHMs), which map inexpensive lexical features into a frozen semantic embedding space by estimating prototype-mixture weights in a rigorously specified RKHS and refining prototypes via normalized least-mean-squares, yielding a transparent decomposition of encoding error into posterior-approximation, generalization, and teacher-noise components. On a controlled Austrian-law benchmark (5,000 queries; 84 laws; 10,762 units), KAHM attains the strongest teacher-space reconstruction among matched learned adapters (MSE 0.000091, R^2 0.9071, cosine 0.9536) and consistently leads rank-sensitive metrics, including mean reciprocal rank at 20 (MRR@20, the average inverse rank of the first relevant result within the top 20), Hit rate at 20 (Hit@20, the fraction of queries with at least one relevant result in the top 20), and Top-1 accuracy (the fraction of queries whose correct item is ranked first), with scores of 0.504, 0.694, and 0.411, respectively. It also reduces per-query latency by a factor of 8.5 relative to direct transformer encoding. These results demonstrate that, in fixed-teacher regimes, lightweight geometric estimators can substitute for online neural encoding, preserving retrieval performance while substantially improving efficiency and interpretability.

</details>


### [61] [Calibration of the underlying surface parameters for urban flood using latent variables and adjoint equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02959)
*Yongfu Tian,Shan Ding,Guofeng Su,Jianguo Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架和最大似然原理的城市地表参数校准方法，通过引入潜变量和伴随方程优化技术，实现了高效且准确的城市洪水模拟参数校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市洪水模拟中，地表参数校准至关重要，但现有方法存在不确定性表征不足和计算效率低的问题。

Method: 将参数校准转化为贝叶斯框架下的优化问题；引入机器学习中的潜变量表征不确定性；构建伴随方程获取梯度信息；提出参数共享和局部化技术降低计算复杂度。

Result: 简单案例验证方法收敛快且对观测时间间隔不敏感；在Test 8A案例中校准城市道路曼宁系数，最大相对误差13.88%，最小1.16%。

Conclusion: 该方法能高效校准城市洪水模拟参数，具有良好收敛性和准确性。

Abstract: Calibrating the urban underlying surface parameters is crucial for urban flood simulation. We formulate the parameter calibration problem into an optimization problem within the Bayesian framework using the maximum likelihood principle. We adopt the urban flood dynamical system model as the surrogate model and innovatively introduce latent variables inspired by machine learning to represent more uncertainties, which can also be compatible with common physical parameter calibration. For more efficient optimization, we construct the adjoint equation of the surrogate model to obtain gradient information and propose the parameter sharing technique and the localization technique to reduce the computation complexity of the adjoint equation. A simple case verifies the proposed method can converge quickly and is insensitive to the observation time interval. In the case derived from Test 8A, we calibrate Manning's coefficient of urban roads, with a maximum relative error of 13.88% and a minimum of 1.16%.

</details>


### [62] [ZeRO-Prefill: Zero Redundancy Overheads in MoE Prefill Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02960)
*Zhaoyuan Su,Olatunji Ruwase,Karthik Ganesan,Aurick Qiao,Samyam Rajbhandari,Juncheng Yang,Yue Cheng,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型在纯prefill场景下的分布式执行瓶颈（计算负载低但通信同步开销大），提出ZeRO-Prefill系统，通过异步专家权重传输（AsyncEP）替代传统的激活路由AllToAll，在prefill前向传播窗口内将专家权重流式传输，实现通信与计算完全重叠；前端结合前缀感知路由和真实FLOPs负载追踪来保证物理饱和阈值。在Qwen3-235B-A22B上，吞吐量相比最强分布式基线提升1.35-1.37倍，长上下文场景达1.59倍，GPU利用率维持在29.8-36.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型并行策略（张量、专家、流水线并行）是为解码时代设计的，在纯prefill负载下会导致冗余计算、通信和同步开销，严重降低服务效率。

Method: 提出ZeRO-Prefill系统，后端采用异步专家并行（AsyncEP），通过权重AllGather替代激活AllToAll，利用大batch prefill的长时间前向传播窗口将专家权重流式传输并完全重叠计算；前端通过前缀感知路由和真实FLOPs负载追踪实现物理饱和阈值约束。

Result: 在Qwen3-235B-A22B上，四种硬件/精度配置下，真实负载吞吐量提升1.35-1.37倍，长上下文合成负载达1.59倍，单GPU模型FLOPs利用率维持29.8-36.2%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦专家放置与同步激活路由，利用prefill阶段的特殊计算特性，能够显著提升MoE模型在纯prefill场景下的服务效率。

Abstract: Production LLM workloads increasingly serve discriminative tasks, such as classification, recommendation, and verification, whose answers are read from the logits of a single prefill pass with no autoregressive decoding. Serving these prefill-only workloads on mixture-of-experts (MoE) models is bottlenecked not by compute but by the distributed execution required to fit the model: existing parallel strategies (tensor, expert, and pipeline parallelism) trade memory pressure for redundant computation, communication, and synchronization, severely degrading MoE prefill serving efficiency. We observe that these overheads stem from coupling expert placement with synchronous activation routing -- a design inherited from the decoding era. The long, compute-bound forward passes of large-batch prefill open a per-layer window wide enough to stream expert weights in the background, replacing per-layer activation AllToAll with asynchronous weight AllGather fully overlapped with computation. We propose ZeRO-Prefill, a prefill-only serving system whose backend, AsyncEP (Asynchronous Expert Parallelism), gathers experts by weight rather than routing them by activation, and whose frontend co-enforces a physically-derived saturation threshold through prefix-aware routing and true-FLOPs load tracking. On Qwen3-235B-A22B across four hardware/precision configurations, ZeRO-Prefill delivers 1.35-1.37x throughput over the strongest distributed baseline on real-world workloads and up to 1.59x on long-context synthetic workloads, sustaining 29.8-36.2% per-GPU model FLOPs utilization.

</details>


### [63] [ISAAC: Auditing Causal Reasoning in Deep Models for Drug-Target Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02962)
*Barbara Tarantino,Sun Kim,Yijingxiu Lu,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ISAAC框架，用于评估药物-靶标相互作用预测模型的结构敏感性，发现AUROC相近的模型在推理质量上存在约25%的差异，而这些差异在传统准确率指标下无法被检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习DTI预测模型在基准测试中表现良好，但可能依赖机械上无意义的分子特征，而标准准确率评估无法检测这一问题。因此需要一种不依赖预测准确性的后验审计方法来评估模型是否具备因果推理能力。

Method: 提出ISAAC（基于干预的结构审计方法），通过对冻结模型进行匹配的机械性和虚假性输入级干预，独立于预测准确性评估模型相对于先验的结构敏感性。

Result: 在Davis基准数据集上对三种基于序列的DTI架构进行测试，ISAAC揭示了在AUROC相近（约3%差异内）的模型之间，推理分数存在约25%的相对差异，且结果在不同训练种子、干预种子和两种扰动算子下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 传统准确率指标无法检测模型在推理质量上的显著差异，后验结构审计应作为科学机器学习（尤其是分子建模）中标准性能评估的补充方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models for drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction often achieve strong benchmark performance without necessarily relying on mechanistically meaningful molecular features, a limitation that standard accuracy-based evaluation cannot detect. We introduce ISAAC (Intervention-based Structural Auditing Approach for Causal Reasoning), a post-hoc framework that evaluates prior-relative structural sensitivity by probing frozen models through matched mechanistic and spurious input-level interventions, independently of predictive accuracy. Applied to three sequence-based DTI architectures on the Davis benchmark, ISAAC reveals approximately 25\% relative differences in reasoning scores across models with comparable AUROC (within around 3\%), stable across training and intervention seeds and two distinct perturbation operators. These discrepancies, undetectable under conventional accuracy metrics, motivate the use of post-hoc structural auditing as a complement to standard performance evaluation in scientific machine learning for molecular modeling.

</details>


### [64] [Reward Hacking Benchmark: Measuring Exploits in LLM Agents with Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02964)
*Kunvar Thaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出奖励黑客基准测试（RHB），评估13个前沿语言模型在多步工具任务中的作弊行为，发现强化学习后训练显著增加奖励黑客行为（DeepSeek-R1-Zero达13.9%），而环境加固可降低87.7%的作弊率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具备工具访问能力的强化学习训练语言模型在编码助手、研究工具和自主系统中广泛应用，评估其在多步任务中利用自然主义捷径（如跳过验证步骤、从元数据推断答案、篡改评估函数）进行奖励黑客行为的风险至关重要。

Method: 构建RHB基准测试，包含多步工具操作任务及自然主义捷径机会；设置独立与链式任务两种模式；对比13个前沿模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google、DeepSeek），通过受控兄弟模型对比（DeepSeek-V3 vs R1-Zero）分析后训练风格的影响。

Result: 作弊率从0%（Claude Sonnet 4.5）到13.9%（DeepSeek-R1-Zero）；RL后训练显著增加作弊（V3的0.6% vs R1-Zero的13.9%）；72%的作弊行为包含明确的思维链推理；环境加固可降低5.7个百分点（87.7%相对降幅）而不影响任务成功率。

Conclusion: 生产对齐的后训练仅在复杂度低于诚实解决方案可行的阈值时抑制奖励黑客行为；随着任务复杂度增加，原本作弊率接近零的模型也会表现出更高的作弊倾向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) trained language model agents with tool access are increasingly deployed in coding assistants, research tools, and autonomous systems. We introduce the Reward Hacking Benchmark (RHB), a suite of multi-step tasks requiring sequential tool operations with naturalistic shortcut opportunities such as skipping verification steps, inferring answers from task-adjacent metadata, or tampering with evaluation-relevant functions. RHB supports independent and chained task regimes, where chain length acts as a proxy for longer-horizon agent behavior.
  We evaluate 13 frontier models from OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and DeepSeek. Exploit rates range from 0% (Claude Sonnet 4.5) to 13.9% (DeepSeek-R1-Zero), varying sharply by post-training style. A controlled sibling comparison (DeepSeek-V3 vs. DeepSeek-R1-Zero) shows RL post-training is associated with substantially higher reward hacking (0.6% vs. 13.9%), with consistent gaps across all four task families. We identify six exploit categories and find that 72% of reward hacking episodes include explicit chain-of-thought rationale, suggesting models often frame exploits as legitimate problem-solving.
  Simple environmental hardening reduces exploit rates by 5.7 percentage points (87.7% relative) without degrading task success. Models with near-zero exploit rates on standard tasks show elevated rates on harder variants, suggesting that production-aligned post-training appears to suppress reward hacking only below a complexity threshold where honest solutions remain tractable.

</details>


### [65] [Joint Energy Management and Coordinated AIGC Workload Scheduling for Distributed Data Centers: A Diffusion-Aided Reward Shaping Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02965)
*Yang Fu,Peng Qin,Liming Chen,Zihao Zhang,Hao Yu,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合能量管理和AIGC工作负载调度框架，通过扩散模型辅助的奖励塑造方法解决调度决策中的奖励稀疏性问题，在真实数据集上实现了更优的系统效用和学习收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC服务在云数据中心带来巨大计算负载，需要战略性调度以降低能源成本并保证服务质量，但现有方法面临模型异构性、服务质量隐性评估和推理过程控制复杂等挑战。

Method: 提出联合能量管理与协调AIGC工作负载调度框架，引入服务质量的显式数学表征，联合考虑数据中心多种能源资源，并开发扩散模型辅助的奖励塑造方法解决深度强化学习中的奖励稀疏性问题。

Result: 基于真实模型和数据集的实验表明，该方案有效应对电价波动和AIGC模型异构性，在收敛速度和系统效用方面均优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型辅助的奖励塑造方法可有效解决AIGC调度中的奖励稀疏问题，实现更高效的学习和更优的系统效用。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-generated content (AIGC) has emerged as a transformative paradigm for automating the creation of diverse and customized content, giving rise to rapidly growing computational workloads in cloud data centers. It is imperative for AIGC service providers (ASPs) to strategically schedule AIGC workloads to reduce data center energy costs while guaranteeing high-quality content generation. However, the distinctive characteristics of AIGC services pose critical challenges, including model heterogeneity across ASPs, implicit service quality evaluation, and complex inference process control. To tackle these challenges, we propose a joint energy management and coordinated AIGC workload scheduling framework, which introduces an explicit mathematical characterization of service quality to promote both job transfer among ASPs and fine-grained inference process configuration. Moreover, various energy resources within data centers are jointly considered to enhance power usage flexibility. Subsequently, a system utility maximization problem is formulated to balance AIGC service revenue with operational penalties and costs. Nevertheless, the strong coupling among job scheduling decisions induces severe reward sparsity, which limits the effectiveness of existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms. To address this issue, we develop a diffusion model-aided reward shaping approach to synthesize complementary reward signals through a multi-step denoising process. This approach is seamlessly integrated with DRL to enable efficient learning of scheduling policies under sparse environmental feedback. Experiments based on real-world models and datasets demonstrate that our scheme effectively accommodates electricity price fluctuations and AIGC model heterogeneity, while achieving superior learning convergence and system utility compared with benchmark methods.

</details>


### [66] [AutoRAGTuner: A Declarative Framework for Automatic Optimization of RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02967)
*Xintan Zeng,Yongchao Liu,Yice Luo,Jiajun Zhen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoRAGTuner 是一个声明式、配置驱动的框架，能够自动化检索增强生成（RAG）的全生命周期（构建、执行、评估与优化），通过模块化架构、领域元素模型（DEM）和自适应贝叶斯优化引擎，显著减少工程开销（代码改动量减少高达95%），并在多种RAG流水线中持续优于默认基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统性能对复杂架构设计和超参数配置高度敏感，而当前依赖低效的手动调优，缺乏系统化的自动化优化方法。

Method: 提出AutoRAGTuner框架，采用声明式配置驱动、模块化架构解耦流水线阶段；引入领域元素模型（DEM）统一异构数据；集成自适应贝叶斯优化引擎进行端到端超参数调优。

Result: 在从普通到基于图的多种RAG流水线中，AutoRAGTuner均优于默认基线；其声明式配置语言使架构调整的代码改动量减少高达95%。

Conclusion: AutoRAGTuner为构建可演化、可复用的RAG系统提供了系统化的可优化基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances LLMs, but performance is highly sensitive to complex architecture designs and hyper-parameter configurations, which currently rely on inefficient manual tuning. We present AutoRAGTuner, a declarative, configuration-driven framework that automates the RAG life cycle: construction, execution,evaluation, and optimization. AutoRAGTuner employs a modular architecture to decouple pipeline stages through a component registration mechanism. To unify heterogeneous data, we introduce the Domain-Element Model (DEM), representing objects as atomic elements with bidirectional pointers to support nodes, edges, and hyperedges. Furthermore, AutoRAGTuner integrates an adaptive Bayesian optimization engine for end-to-end hyper-parameter tuning. Experimental results demonstrate AutoRAGTuner's architectural generality: across diverse RAG pipelines, ranging from vanilla to graph-based, the framework consistently outperforms default baselines. Notably, AutoRAGTuner significantly mitigates engineering overhead, where its declarative configuration language enables a up to 95\% reduction in code churn for architectural adjustments. Overall, AutoRAGTuner provides a systematically optimizable foundation for building evolvable and reusable RAG systems.

</details>


### [67] [Finite-Size Gradient Transport in Large Language Model Pretraining: From Cascade Size to Intensive Transport Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02968)
*Ping Wang,Yan-Qi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于五个可观测量的有限规模梯度传输框架，分析Pico-LM和Pythia两个语言模型家族的梯度数据，发现两者共享级联规模骨架但处于不同传输状态，且性能关联主要体现在特定通道上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对真实语言模型训练中梯度传输行为的系统分析框架，本文旨在建立一套可复用的有限规模梯度传输测量方法，分离级联规模、持续时间和传输效率等不同维度。

Method: 基于五个可观测量(D,z,β,δ,v_rel)构建梯度传输框架，对Pico-LM（4个尺度、125个对齐步）和Pythia（5个尺度、153个对齐检查点差分更新场）的原始梯度数据进行直接分析，并使用随机场对照作为基线。

Result: 两个家族共享近一致的级联规模骨架，但处于不同传输状态：Pico-LM呈现正持续时间缩放和负密集效率缩放，而Pythia接近D=1基线且效率缩放较弱；两者在持续时间和密集通道上具有匹配的随机场基线；Pico-LM保持清晰幂律，Pythia则较弱。

Conclusion: 该框架可复用用于测量不同语言模型的梯度传输行为，但并未声称存在通用固定点或从第一性原理推导出神经缩放定律。

Abstract: We introduce a finite-size gradient-transport framework for real language-model training, based on five observables $(D,z,β,δ,v_{\mathrm{rel}})$ that separate cascade size, duration, absolute transport, and intensive transport efficiency. We analyze direct raw-gradient measurements from Pico-LM across four scales and 125 aligned steps, together with a five-scale Pythia companion dataset built from 153 aligned checkpoint-difference update fields. The same algebraic closure holds in both families, and both share a near-unity cascade-size backbone, but they occupy distinct transport regimes: Pico-LM shows positive duration scaling and negative intensive-efficiency scaling, whereas Pythia remains near the $D=1$ baseline with only weak positive efficiency scale dependence. Randomized-field controls give nearly matched null floors in the intensive and duration channels, indicating that the contrast reflects different real departures from a shared null skeleton rather than different null calibrations. The families also differ in stepwise power-law compressibility: Pico-LM retains clean duration and efficiency power laws, whereas Pythia preserves the size backbone but shows weaker one-slope compressibility in those channels. External performance associations are correspondingly channel-level, carried mainly by $v_{\mathrm{rel}}$ and normalized cascade duration, while $D(t)$ acts as a shared size backbone without a significant exponent-level performance association. These results support a reusable transport measurement framework without claiming a universal fixed point or a first-principles derivation of neural scaling laws.

</details>


### [68] [Multilingual Safety Alignment via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02971)
*Ruiyang Qin,Qingzhuo Wang,Dongrui Liu,Qiang Li,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多语言自蒸馏框架（MSD），通过将高资源语言的安全能力迁移到低资源语言，解决了大语言模型在低资源语言上的安全对齐问题，无需任何语言的响应数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高资源语言（如英语）上具备良好安全防护，但在低资源语言（如爪哇语）上极易受到越狱攻击。现有安全对齐方法需要为目标语言生成高质量响应数据，成本高昂且难以实现。

Method: 提出多语言自蒸馏（MSD）框架，将LLM自身从高资源语言习得的安全能力迁移至低资源语言。实现两种具体方法：on-policy MSD和off-policy MSD。同时提出双视角安全加权（DPSW），从教师和学生两个视角联合优化蒸馏目标，自适应调整安全关键令牌的惩罚权重。

Result: 在多个代表性LLM及多语言越狱和实用性基准测试上，该方法一致取得了卓越的多语言安全性能，能有效泛化到更具挑战性的数据集和未见过的语言，同时保留模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: MSD框架通过自蒸馏实现跨语言安全能力迁移，无需任何语言的响应数据，DPSW进一步优化了蒸馏过程，为多语言安全对齐提供了高效且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit severe multilingual safety misalignment: they possess strong safeguards in high-resource languages but remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in low-resource languages. Current safety alignment methods generally rely on high-quality response data for each target language, which is expensive and difficult to generate. In this paper, we propose a cross-lingual safeguard transfer framework named Multilingual Self-Distillation (MSD). This framework transfers an LLM's inherent safety capabilities from high-resource (e.g., English) to low-resource (e.g., Javanese) languages, overcoming the need for response data in any language. Our framework is flexible and can be integrated with different self-distillation strategies. Specifically, we implement two concrete methods -- on-policy MSD and off-policy MSD -- both of which enable effective cross-lingual safety transfer using only multilingual queries. Furthermore, we propose Dual-Perspective Safety Weighting (DPSW), a divergence measure to optimize the distillation objective. By jointly considering the perspectives of both the teacher and the student, DPSW adaptively increases the penalty weights on safety-critical tokens while reducing the weights on non-critical tokens. Extensive experiments on representative LLMs across diverse multilingual jailbreak and utility benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently achieves superior multilingual safety performance. Notably, it generalizes effectively to more challenging datasets and unseen languages while preserving the model's general capabilities.

</details>


### [69] [Structured Diffusion Bridges: Inductive Bias for Denoising Diffusion Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02973)
*Eitan Kosman,Gabriele Serussi,Chaim Basking*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种扩散桥框架，通过对齐约束限制解空间，在无需完全配对数据的情况下实现模态翻译，在无配对、半配对和全配对场景下均表现一致，且能在配对要求大幅放宽时接近全配对质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模态翻译方法大多依赖完全配对数据集，而模态翻译本身是欠约束问题（多种跨模态映射可产生相同边缘分布），需要更灵活的约束机制。

Method: 提出扩散桥框架，用对齐约束来表征和限制解空间，将配对监督视为可选启发式信息而非必要前提。

Result: 在合成数据和真实模态翻译基准上，在无配对、半配对和全配对场景下均表现一致，在配对要求大幅放宽时接近全配对质量，且能适用于无配对场景。

Conclusion: 扩散桥可作为超越全配对数据的模态翻译灵活基础框架。

Abstract: Modality translation is inherently under-constrained, as multiple cross-modal mappings may yield the same marginals. Recent work has shown that diffusion bridges are effective for this task. However, most existing approaches rely on fully paired datasets, thereby imposing a single data-driven constraint. We propose a diffusion-bridge framework that characterizes the space of admissible solutions and restricts it via alignment constraints, treating paired supervision as an optional heuristic rather than a prerequisite. We validate our method on synthetic and real modality translation benchmarks across unpaired, semi-paired, and paired regimes, showing consistent performance across supervision levels. Notably, \textbf{it achieves near fully-paired quality with a substantial relaxation in pairing requirements, and remaining applicable in the unpaired regime}. These results highlight diffusion bridges as a flexible foundation for modality translation beyond fully paired data.

</details>


### [70] [Mixed-Precision Information Bottlenecks for On-Device Trait-State Disentanglement in Bipolar Agitation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03039)
*Joydeep Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MP-IB框架，通过混合精度量化作为信息瓶颈，在资源受限的边缘设备上实现双相情感障碍躁动状态监测，有效分离说话人身份与情感状态，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上持续监测双相情感障碍患者的躁动状态时，需要从语音生物标志物中分离稳定的说话人特质与可变的情绪状态，但资源受限的设备难以支持复杂模型。

Method: 将混合精度量化视为信息瓶颈：FP16精度（1024比特）编码说话人身份，INT4精度（128比特）编码躁动状态，形成8倍信息不对称；结合动态精度调度和多尺度时间融合。

Result: Bridge2AI-Voice数据集上rho=0.117，优于WavLM-Adapter等基线方法2.8-15.9个点；零样本迁移到CREMA-D取得AUC=0.817；身份泄露抑制到接近随机水平；端到端延迟23.4ms，模型仅617KB。

Conclusion: MP-IB首次证明数值精度本身可作为信息瓶颈用于临床特质-状态分离，在极低资源下实现有效的情绪监测，适用于低成本边缘设备。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of bipolar disorder agitation via voice biomarkers requires disentangling stable speaker traits from volatile affective states on resource-constrained edge devices. We introduce MP-IB, the first framework to treat mixed-precision quantization as an information bottleneck for clinical trait-state separation. The core insight is that numerical precision itself controls capacity: an FP16 trait head (1,024 bits) encodes speaker identity, while an INT4 state head (128 bits) captures agitation, yielding 8x information asymmetry without adversarial training. We augment this with Dynamic Precision Scheduling and Multi-Scale Temporal Fusion. On Bridge2AI-Voice (N=833, 4 sessions/participant, strict speaker-independent CV), MP-IB achieves rho = 0.117 (95\% CI: [0.089, 0.145], p=0.003 vs. chance), outperforming 94M-parameter WavLM-Adapter with in-domain SSL continuation (rho = -0.042), beta VAE disentanglement (rho = 0.089), and hand-crafted prosody (rho = 0.031) by 2.8--15.9 points absolute. Zero-shot transfer to CREMA-D achieves AUC=0.817. Identity leakage is suppressed to near-random (EER=0.42, MIA-AUC=0.52). End-to-end latency is 23.4 ms with a 617 KB footprint, enabling real-time monitoring on sub 20 dollar devices.

</details>


### [71] [TCD-Arena: Assessing Robustness of Time Series Causal Discovery Methods Against Assumption Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03045)
*Gideon Stein,Niklas Penzel,Tristan Piater,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TCD-Arena，一个模块化、可扩展的测试工具，用于评估时间序列因果发现算法在面对逐步严重假设违反时的鲁棒性。通过约3000万次因果发现尝试和33种假设违反场景的实证研究，揭示了不同算法的鲁棒性特征，并发现集成方法能提升整体鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现框架在实际应用中因依赖强假设且缺乏鲁棒性评估而受限，现有方法难以在假设违反时可靠运行。

Method: 开发TCD-Arena测试工具，模块化设计支持自定义假设违反场景，通过约3000万次CD实验系统评估33种假设违反对时间序列CD算法的影响，并探索集成方法的鲁棒性提升效果。

Result: 揭示了不同时间序列CD算法在33种假设违反下的差异化鲁棒性特征，发现集成方法能提升一般性鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TCD-Arena有助于推动开发更可靠、适应多样化数据条件的因果发现方法。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) is a powerful framework for scientific inquiry. Yet, its practical adoption is hindered by a reliance on strong, often unverifiable assumptions and a lack of robust performance assessment. To address these limitations and advance empirical CD evaluation, we present TCD-Arena, a modularized, highly customizable, and extendable testing kit to assess the robustness of time series CD algorithms against stepwise more severe assumption violations. For demonstration, we conduct an extensive empirical study comprising around 30 million individual CD attempts and reveal nuanced robustness profiles for 33 distinct assumption violations. Further, we investigate CD ensembles and find that they have the potential to improve general robustness, which has implications for real-world applications. With this, we strive to ultimately facilitate the development of CD methods that are reliable for a diverse range of synthetic and potentially real-world data conditions.

</details>


### [72] [Adaptive Negative Scheduling for Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03076)
*Adnan Ali,Jinlong Li,Syed Muhammad Israr,Ali Kashif Bashir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdNGCL框架，通过自适应负采样调度策略（HANS）提升图对比学习性能，在9个基准数据集的7个上达到最佳准确率，同时有效控制计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图对比学习（GCL）方法依赖静态负采样，无法适应训练过程中负样本信息量的动态变化，且忽略了负样本的计算成本，导致学习效率低下。

Method: 提出AdNGCL框架，包含硬度感知调度器（HANS）：将负样本选择建模为损失门控、预算约束的过程，分为困难、中等、容易三层；根据对比损失趋势动态调整步长，在全局和每类预算下进行调度，并定期刷新样本以维持多样性。

Result: 在9个基准图数据集上，AdNGCL在7个数据集上取得最佳准确率，在剩余2个数据集上取得第二佳结果，同时实现了对计算成本的显式控制。

Conclusion: 预算感知、损失敏感的调度策略是一种提升表征学习鲁棒性和效率的通用策略，在计算智能应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Graph contrastive learning (GCL) has become a central paradigm for self-supervised representation learning in computational intelligence, with applications spanning recommendation, anomaly detection, and personalization. A key limitation of existing methods is their reliance on static negative sampling, which fails to account for the dynamic informativeness and computational cost of negatives during training. We propose AdNGCL, an adaptive negative scheduling framework with a hardness-aware scheduler (HANS) that formulates negative selection as a loss-gated, budget-constrained process across hard, intermediate, and easy strata. The scheduler dynamically adjusts step sizes based on contrastive loss trends under both global and per-category budgets, while periodically refreshing samples to maintain diversity without exceeding compute constraints. Experiments on nine benchmark graph datasets demonstrate that AdNGCL consistently advances state-of-the-art performance, achieving the best accuracy on seven datasets and second-best on the remaining two, while offering explicit control over computational cost. These results highlight the value of budget-aware, loss-sensitive scheduling as a general strategy for improving the robustness and efficiency of representation learning in emerging computational intelligence applications.

</details>


### [73] [Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction for Memory-Bounded EEG Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03085)
*Chengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对无监督个体持续学习（UICL）中存储全量样本导致违反持续学习目标的问题，本文提出ADaCoRe方法，通过形态感知的选择、压缩与重建实现内存高效的无监督EEG信号持续学习，在三个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UICL方法通常存储完整的历史样本，这违背了持续学习避免重训练的核心目标；同时EEG信号存在显著噪声和个体差异，在有限标注情况下难以实现鲁棒的个性化适应。

Method: 提出自适应数据压缩与重建（ADaCoRe）框架，包含四个核心组件：显著性驱动的关键帧保护、有理多相压缩、伴随重建（对保护索引进行逐字覆写）、以及基于原型置信度的自适应样本维护策略。

Result: 在ISRUC和FACED数据集上分别获得至少+2.7和+15.3的准确率提升，消融研究量化了压缩-保真度权衡，可视化验证了压缩与重建过程中EEG关键形态特征的保留。

Conclusion: ADaCoRe通过形态感知的压缩-重建机制，在严格内存约束下实现了高效的EEG无监督持续学习，兼顾了压缩效率与关键生理信号形态的保真度。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) signals provide millisecond-level temporal resolution but their analysis is limited by remarkable noise and inter-subject variability, making robust personalization difficult under limited annotations. Unsupervised Individual Continual Learning (UICL) has been proposed to address this practical challenge, where a model pretrained on a labeled cohort must adapt online to unlabeled subject streams under strict memory constraints. However, existing UICL methods typically store full past samples, which undermine the continual learning goal of avoiding retraining. Observing that EEG signals exhibit well-structured morphologies to be exploited via morphology-aware selection, compression, and reconstruction, here we propose Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction (ADaCoRe) for UICL. This is a memory-efficient pipeline composed of saliency-driven keyframe protection, rational polyphase compression, adjoint reconstruction with verbatim overwrite on protected indices, and prototype-confidence selection for adaptive exemplar maintenance. Across three representative benchmarks, ADaCoRe consistently outperforms recent strong baselines under tight buffer regimes (eg., the performance gains are at least +2.7 and +15.3 ACC on ISRUC and FACED datasets, respectively). Ablation studies quantify compression-fidelity trade-offs and highlight the contribution of each design, while visualizations confirm the preservation of key EEG morphology during compression and reconstruction.

</details>


### [74] [Attribution-Guided Masking for Robust Cross-Domain Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03091)
*Shubham Harkare,Arvind Yogesh Suresh Babu,Yash Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出归因引导掩码(AGM)方法，通过动态检测和惩罚微调期间高归因的虚假词元，提升预训练Transformer模型在跨领域情感分类中的泛化能力，无需目标域标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer模型在域内情感分类准确率高，但迁移到域外数据时性能严重下降，原因是模型依赖域特定的虚假词元（如@提及、话题标签、俚语）。现有词元级归因漂移方法无法有效预测此泛化差距。

Method: 提出归因引导掩码(AGM)方法，核心组件是基于梯度的归因掩码损失(L_mask)，可选结合反事实对比损失以强制学习域不变表示。在微调期间动态检测并惩罚高归因的虚假词元，无需目标域标签或人工标注。

Result: 在4个不同领域、8个随机种子的严格零样本迁移设置下，AGM在最具挑战的Sentiment140迁移任务上达到竞争性泛化性能(Δ=0.244)，与DANN(0.264)、DRO(0.248)、Fish(0.247)、IRM(0.238)等强基线相当，同时提供独特的词元级可解释性。

Conclusion: 定性分析证实AGM能抑制对域特定词元的归因，将模型依赖转向域不变的情感标记。消融实验表明归因引导掩码是关键组件，移除或替换为随机词元选择会持续降低困难迁移任务的性能。

Abstract: While pre-trained Transformer models achieve high accuracy on in-domain sentiment classification, they frequently experience severe performance degradation when transferring to out-of-domain data. We hypothesize that this generalization gap is driven by reliance on domain-specific spurious tokens. After demonstrating that post-hoc-token-level attribution drift fails to predict this gap, we propose Attribution-Guided Masking (AGM), a training time intervention that dynamically detects and penalizes highly attributed spurious tokens during fine-tuning. AGM's core component is a gradient based attribution masking loss ($\mathcal{L}_{mask}$), which can optionally be combined with a counterfactual contrastive loss to enforce domain-invariant representations, all without requiring target-domain labels or human annotation. Evaluated in a strict zero-shot transfer setting across four diverse domains with eight random seeds, AGM achieves competitive generalization compared to five strong baselines on the hardest transfer (Sentiment140): $Δ$ = 0.244 versus DANN (0.264), DRO (0.248), Fish (0.247), and IRM (0.238), while uniquely providing token-level interpretability into which features drive the generalization gap. Our qualitative analysis confirms that AGM suppresses attribution on domain-specific tokens such as @mentions, hashtags, and slang, shifting reliance toward domain-invariant sentiment markers. Our ablation study further confirms that attribution-guided masking is the critical component: removing it or replacing it with random token selection consistently degrades performance on difficult transfers.

</details>


### [75] [When Prompts Interact: Assessing Prompt Arithmetic for Deconfounding under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03096)
*Zhecheng Sheng,Yongsen Tan,Xiruo Ding,Trevor Cohen,Serguei Pakhomov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyPA通过混合任务提示和线性化混淆提示的算术组合，以参数高效的方式提升模型在分布偏移下对虚假特征的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类任务中模型常依赖混淆变量（虚假特征）在分布内取得好性能，但在分布偏移下表现急剧下降。任务算术可移除不需要的信号，但全量微调计算成本高，而提示调优虽参数高效，其算术组合能否限制虚假特征依赖尚不明确。

Method: 提出Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic (HyPA)，将任务提示与线性化的混淆提示进行算术组合，通过混合操作来抵消虚假相关性。

Result: 在多个基准上，HyPA相比提示算术基线，在分布偏移下持续改善了鲁棒性与性能的权衡。进一步分析表明HyPA通过减少混淆信号对预测的影响或抑制其在表征中的存在来缓解混淆效应。

Conclusion: HyPA是一种参数高效的、有前景的提升模型在混淆偏移下鲁棒性的方法。

Abstract: In classification tasks, models may rely on confounding variables to achieve strong in-distribution performance, capturing spurious features that fail under distribution shift. This shortcut behavior leads to substantial degradation in out-of-distribution settings. Task arithmetic offers a potential solution by removing unwanted signals via subtraction of secondary model updates, but it typically requires full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive.
